// ---------------- RNG (seeded deterministic) ----------------
function cyrb128(str){
  let h1=1779033703,h2=3144134277,h3=1013904242,h4=2773480762;
  for(let i=0,k;i<str.length;i++){k=str.charCodeAt(i); h1=h2^Math.imul(h1^k,597399067); h2=h3^Math.imul(h2^k,2869860233); h3=h4^Math.imul(h3^k,951274213); h4=h1^Math.imul(h4^k,2716044179);}
  h1=Math.imul(h3^(h1>>>18),597399067); h2=Math.imul(h4^(h2>>>22),2869860233); h3=Math.imul(h1^(h3>>>17),951274213); h4=Math.imul(h2^(h4>>>19),2716044179);
  return [(h1^h2^h3^h4)>>>0,(h2^h1)>>>0,(h3^h1)>>>0,(h4^h1)>>>0];
}
function sfc32(a,b,c,d){ return function(){ a|=0;b|=0;c|=0;d|=0; let t=(a+b|0)+d|0; d=d+1|0; a=b^b>>>9; b=c+(c<<3)|0; c=(c<<21|c>>>11); c=c+t|0; return (t>>>0)/4294967296; } }
function makeRNG(seedStr){ const s=cyrb128(seedStr); return sfc32(s[0],s[1],s[2],s[3]); }
// ---------------- Input Manager ----------------
class InputManager{
  constructor(canvas){
    this.canvas=canvas;
    this.keys={};
    this.keyDown={};
    this.mouse={ x:0,y:0, left:false, right:false, worldX:0, worldY:0 };
    this.joy={ active:false, x:0, y:0 };
    this.touchIds={ move:null, attack:null };
    this.buffer={ attack:0, dodge:0 };
    this.bind();
    this.preventScrolling();
  }
  bind(){
    window.addEventListener('keydown', e=>{
      const k=e.key.toLowerCase();
      if(['arrowup','arrowdown','arrowleft','arrowright',' ','space','enter'].includes(k) || k===' '){ /* prevent scroll handled elsewhere */ }
      if(!this.keys[k]) this.keyDown[k]=true;
      this.keys[k]=true;
      if([' ','arrowup','arrowdown','arrowleft','arrowright'].includes(e.key)) e.preventDefault();
    });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', e=>{
      const k=e.key.toLowerCase();
      this.keys[k]=false;
    });
    // mouse
    this.canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', e=>{
      if(e.button===0) this.mouse.left=true;
      if(e.button===2) this.mouse.right=true;
      this.updateMouse(e);
    });
    window.addEventListener('mouseup', e=>{
      if(e.button===0) this.mouse.left=false;
      if(e.button===2) this.mouse.right=false;
    });
    this.canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', e=> this.updateMouse(e));
    this.canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', e=> e.preventDefault());
    // touch joystick + buttons handled separately
  }
  preventScrolling(){
    document.addEventListener('touchmove', e=>{ if(e.target.closest('#gameContainer')) e.preventDefault(); }, {passive:false});
  }
  updateMouse(e){
    const rect=this.canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    const sx=this.canvas.width/rect.width, sy=this.canvas.height/rect.height;
    this.mouse.x=(e.clientX-rect.left)*sx;
    this.mouse.y=(e.clientY-rect.top)*sy;
  }
  // call each frame
  update(dt, camera){
    // buffer timers
    if(this.buffer.attack>0) this.buffer.attack-=dt;
    if(this.buffer.dodge>0) this.buffer.dodge-=dt;
    if(this.keyDown['j'] || this.mouse.left) this.buffer.attack=CONFIG.player.attackBuffer;
    if(this.keyDown[' '] || this.keys[' '] || this.keyDown['space']) this.buffer.dodge=CONFIG.player.attackBuffer;
    // consume keyDown one-shot
    for(let k in this.keyDown) this.keyDown[k]=false;
    if(this.mouse.left) this.mouse.left=false; // one-shot for queue, but hold also? we'll treat as buffer
    // world mouse
    if(camera){
      this.mouse.worldX = (this.mouse.x - this.canvas.width/2)/camera.zoom + camera.x;
      this.mouse.worldY = (this.mouse.y - this.canvas.height/2)/camera.zoom + camera.y;
    }
  }
  moveVector(){
    let x=0,y=0;
    if(this.keys['w']||this.keys['arrowup']) y-=1;
    if(this.keys['s']||this.keys['arrowdown']) y+=1;
    if(this.keys['a']||this.keys['arrowleft']) x-=1;
    if(this.keys['d']||this.keys['arrowright']) x+=1;
    if(this.joy.active){
      x+=this.joy.x;
      y+=this.joy.y;
    }
    const len=Math.hypot(x,y);
    if(len>1){ x/=len; y/=len; }
    return {x,y,len:Math.min(1,len)};
  }
  consumeAttack(){ if(this.buffer.attack>0){ this.buffer.attack=0; return true; } return false; }
  consumeDodge(){ if(this.buffer.dodge>0){ this.buffer.dodge=0; return true; } return false; }
}
// ---------------- Audio (procedural WebAudio) ----------------
class AudioManager{
  constructor(){
    this.enabled=true;
    this.ctx=null;
    this.master=null;
    this.sfxEnabled=true;
  }
  ensure(){
    if(this.ctx) return;
    try{
      this.ctx=new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
      this.master=this.ctx.createGain();
      this.master.gain.value=0.42;
      this.master.connect(this.ctx.destination);
    }catch(e){ this.ctx=null; }
  }
  toggle(){ this.enabled=!this.enabled; if(this.master) this.master.gain.value=this.enabled?0.42:0; return this.enabled; }
  play(type){
    if(!this.enabled||!this.sfxEnabled) return;
    this.ensure();
    if(!this.ctx) return;
    const t=this.ctx.currentTime;
    const o=this.ctx.createOscillator();
    const g=this.ctx.createGain();
    o.connect(g); g.connect(this.master);
    let freq=440, dur=0.18, vol=0.5, oscType='sine';
    switch(type){
      case 'attack': freq=220+Math.random()*80; dur=0.12; vol=0.45; oscType='sawtooth'; break;
      case 'hit': freq=120; dur=0.16; vol=0.6; oscType='square'; break;
      case 'crit': freq=640; dur=0.22; vol=0.55; break;
      case 'dodge': freq=520; dur=0.2; vol=0.4; oscType='triangle'; break;
      case 'coin': freq=880; dur=0.18; vol=0.35; break;
      case 'reward': freq=440; dur=0.6; vol=0.5; break;
      case 'roomClear': freq= 330; dur=0.5; vol=0.5; break;
      case 'bossEnter': freq= 90; dur=1.0; vol=0.7; oscType='sawtooth'; break;
      case 'pause': freq= 300; dur=0.12; vol=0.3; break;
      case 'ui': freq= 600; dur=0.08; vol=0.25; break;
      default: freq=440; dur=0.12;
    }
    o.type=oscType; o.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq,t);
    if(type==='hit') o.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(40, t+dur);
    if(type==='attack') o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(freq*1.8, t+0.06);
    if(type==='coin') o.frequency.linearRampToValueAtTime(freq*1.5, t+0.08);
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol,t);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.001, t+dur);
    o.start(t); o.stop(t+dur+0.02);
  }
}
// ---------------- Particles (pooled) ----------------
class ParticlePool{
  constructor(max){
    this.pool=[]; this.active=[];
    for(let i=0;i<max;i++) this.pool.push({ alive:false });
  }
  spawn(opts){
    let p=this.pool.pop();
    if(!p){
      if(this.active.length>=CONFIG.perf.maxParticles) return null;
      p={ alive:false };
    }
    Object.assign(p, opts, { alive:true, life:opts.life||0.6, t:0 });
    this.active.push(p);
    return p;
  }
  update(dt){
    for(let i=this.active.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const p=this.active[i];
      p.t+=dt;
      if(p.t>=p.life || (p.alpha!==undefined && p.alpha<=0)){
        p.alive=false;
        this.active.splice(i,1);
        this.pool.push(p);
      }else{
        if(p.vx!==undefined){ p.x+=p.vx*dt; p.vy+=p.vy*dt; if(p.gravity) p.vy+=p.gravity*dt; }
        if(p.shrink) p.size*=1-p.shrink*dt;
        if(p.fade) p.alpha-=p.fade*dt;
      }
    }
  }
  draw(ctx){
    for(const p of this.active){
      if(p.type==='ember'){
        ctx.globalAlpha=p.alpha!==undefined?p.alpha: 0.9*(1-p.t/p.life);
        ctx.fillStyle=p.color||`rgba(255,${120+Math.random()*80|0},${20+Math.random()*30|0},${ctx.globalAlpha})`;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y, p.size||2, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
        // glow
        ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,122,46,${0.12*ctx.globalAlpha})`;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y, (p.size||2)*3, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }else if(p.type==='spark'){
        ctx.globalAlpha=p.alpha!==undefined?p.alpha: (1-p.t/p.life);
        ctx.fillStyle=p.color||'#ffdf9a';
        ctx.fillRect(p.x,p.y,2,2);
      }else if(p.type==='coinSpark'){
        ctx.globalAlpha=0.9*(1-p.t/p.life);
        ctx.fillStyle='#ffdf5a';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,1.8,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }else if(p.type==='afterimage'){
        ctx.globalAlpha=(1-p.t/p.life)*0.32;
        ctx.fillStyle=p.color||'#a0a0ff';
        ctx.globalAlpha*=0.6;
        // handled by entity draw
      }
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
  }
}
class FloatingTextPool{
  constructor(max){
    this.pool=[]; this.active=[];
    for(let i=0;i<max;i++) this.pool.push({alive:false});
  }
  spawn(x,y,text,color,life=0.9){
    let f=this.pool.pop()||{alive:false};
    f.x=x; f.y=y; f.text=text; f.color=color||'#fff'; f.life=life; f.t=0; f.alive=true; f.vy=-36;
    this.active.push(f);
  }
  update(dt){
    for(let i=this.active.length-1;i>=0;i--){
      const f=this.active[i];
      f.t+=dt; f.y+=f.vy*dt; f.vy+=48*dt;
      if(f.t>=f.life){
        f.alive=false; this.active.splice(i,1); this.pool.push(f);
      }
    }
  }
  draw(ctx){
    for(const f of this.active){
      const a=1-f.t/f.life;
      ctx.globalAlpha=a;
      ctx.fillStyle=f.color;
      ctx.font=`bold ${14+Math.floor(a*4)}px ui-sans-serif, sans-serif`;
      ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText(f.text, f.x, f.y);
      ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    }
  }
}
// ---------------- Entities ----------------
let _eid=1;
class Entity{
  constructor(x,y){ this.id=_eid++; this.x=x; this.y=y; this.vx=0; this.vy=0; this.alive=true; this.radius=16; }
}
class Projectile extends Entity{
  constructor(x,y,vx,vy,damage,owner,life=3.2,radius=6){
    super(x,y); this.vx=vx; this.vy=vy; this.damage=damage; this.owner=owner; this.life=life; this.t=0; this.radius=radius;
  }
  update(dt){
    this.t+=dt; this.x+=this.vx*dt; this.y+=this.vy*dt;
    if(this.t>this.life) this.alive=false;
  }
}
class Coin extends Entity{
  constructor(x,y,value){ super(x,y); this.value=value; this.collected=false; this.t=0; this.magnet=false; this.radius=8; }
  update(dt, player){
    this.t+=dt;
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    if(dist<160) this.magnet=true;
    if(this.magnet){
      const dx=player.x-this.x, dy=player.y-this.y;
      const d=Math.max(1, Math.hypot(dx,dy));
      const pull= 380 * (1 + (160-d)/160);
      this.x+= (dx/d)*pull*dt;
      this.y+= (dy/d)*pull*dt;
    }else{
      // bob
      this.y+= Math.sin(this.t*3)*8*dt;
    }
    if(dist<22) { this.collected=true; this.alive=false; return true; }
    return false;
  }
}
class Player extends Entity{
  constructor(x,y){
    super(x,y);
    this.radius=16;
    this.facing=0; // angle
    this.health=CONFIG.player.health;
    this.maxHealth=CONFIG.player.health;
    this.state='idle';
    this.stateT=0;
    this.dashT=0;
    this.dashDir={x:0,y:0};
    this.iFrameT=0;
    this.attackT=0;
    this.attackPhase='none'; // windup active recovery
    this.hitT=0;
    this.abilityCooldown=0;
    this.emberCharge=0;
    this.coins=0;
    this.score=0;
    this.combo=0;
    this.comboT=0;
    this.relics=[];
    this.hasShard=false;
    this.armor=0;
    this.enemiesDefeated=0;
    this.perfectDodgeWindow=0;
    this.trail=[];
  }
  hasRelic(id){ return this.relics.some(r=>r.id===id); }
  addRelic(relic){
    this.relics.push(relic);
    if(relic.id==='shard') this.hasShard=true;
    if(relic.id==='coin_plate') this.armor+=2;
  }
  takeDamage(d, source){
    if(this.iFrameT>0 || this.dashT>0) { // perfect dodge if within window
      if(this.perfectDodgeWindow>0){
        this.combo++; this.comboT=3.5;
        return 'perfect';
      }
      return 'dodged';
    }
    // armor
    if(this.armor>0){
      const absorb=Math.min(this.armor, Math.ceil(d*0.6));
      this.armor-=absorb; d-=absorb;
    }
    this.health-=d;
    this.hitT=CONFIG.player.hitCooldown;
    this.state='hit'; this.stateT=0;
    if(this.health<=0){ this.alive=false; }
    return 'hit';
  }
  startAttack(facing){
    if(this.attackPhase!=='none') return false;
    this.attackPhase='windup'; this.attackT=0; this.facing=facing; this.state='attack';
    return true;
  }
  startDodge(dir){
    if(this.dashT>0) return false;
    if(this.abilityCooldown>0 && false) {}
    if(this.playerDodgeCooldown>0) return false;
    this.dashT=CONFIG.player.dashDur;
    this.dashDir={x:dir.x, y:dir.y};
    this.iFrameT=CONFIG.player.iFrameDur;
    this.perfectDodgeWindow=0.18;
    this.state='dodge';
    this.dodgeCooldown=CONFIG.player.dodgeCooldown;
    return true;
  }
  tryAbility(facing){
    if(this.abilityCooldown>0) return false;
    this.abilityCooldown=CONFIG.player.abilityCooldown;
    this.facing=facing;
    this.state='ability'; this.stateT=0;
    return true;
  }
  update(dt, input, game){
    this.stateT+=dt;
    if(this.dashT>0){
      this.dashT-=dt;
      this.x+=this.dashDir.x*CONFIG.player.dashSpeed*dt;
      this.y+=this.dashDir.y*CONFIG.player.dashSpeed*dt;
      this.trail.push({x:this.x,y:this.y,t:0.22});
      if(this.dashT<=0){ this.state='idle'; this.perfectDodgeWindow=0; }
    }
    if(this.iFrameT>0) this.iFrameT-=dt;
    if(this.hitT>0) this.hitT-=dt;
    if(this.perfectDodgeWindow>0) this.perfectDodgeWindow-=dt;
    if(this.dodgeCooldown>0) this.dodgeCooldown-=dt;
    if(this.abilityCooldown>0) this.abilityCooldown-=dt;
    if(this.comboT>0){ this.comboT-=dt; if(this.comboT<=0) this.combo=0; }
    // trail fade
    for(let i=this.trail.length-1;i>=0;i--){ this.trail[i].t-=dt; if(this.trail[i].t<=0) this.trail.splice(i,1); }
    // movement if not locked in attack windup/active
    let canMove = this.attackPhase==='none' && this.dashT<=0 && this.state!=='hit';
    if(canMove){
      const mv=input.moveVector();
      if(mv.len>0.05){
        this.x+=mv.x*CONFIG.player.speed*dt*(this.hasRelic('desperate')&&this.health<this.maxHealth*0.45?1.28:1);
        this.y+=mv.y*CONFIG.player.speed*dt*(this.hasRelic('desperate')&&this.health<this.maxHealth*0.45?1.28:1);
        if(mv.len>0.1) this.facing=Math.atan2(mv.y,mv.x);
        this.state='move';
      }else{
        if(this.state==='move') this.state='idle';
      }
      // aim faces mouse if mouse moved? Use mouse world if near
      if(game && Math.hypot(input.mouse.worldX - this.x, input.mouse.worldY - this.y)>40){
        this.facing=Math.atan2(input.mouse.worldY - this.y, input.mouse.worldX - this.x);
      }
    }
    // attack phases
    if(this.attackPhase!=='none'){
      this.attackT+=dt;
      if(this.attackPhase==='windup' && this.attackT>=CONFIG.player.attackWindup){
        this.attackPhase='active'; this.attackT=0;
        // do hit check in game
        game.performPlayerAttack(this);
      }else if(this.attackPhase==='active' && this.attackT>=CONFIG.player.attackActive){
        this.attackPhase='recovery'; this.attackT=0;
      }else if(this.attackPhase==='recovery' && this.attackT>=CONFIG.player.attackRecovery){
        this.attackPhase='none'; this.attackT=0; this.state='idle';
      }
    }
    if(this.state==='ability'){
      if(this.stateT>0.32){
        this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0;
      }
    }
    if(this.state==='hit' && this.stateT>0.18){ this.state='idle'; }
    // clamp to room bounds handled by game
  }
}
// Enemy base
class Enemy extends Entity{
  constructor(x,y,type){
    super(x,y); this.type=type; this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0; this.health=60; this.maxHealth=60;
    this.damage=10; this.speed=70; this.windupT=0; this.cooldown=0; this.hitT=0;
    this.alive=true; this.intent=0; this.bodyRadius=18; this.isElite=false; this.facing=0;
  }
  takeDamage(d, fromPlayer, isCrit=false){
    this.health-=d; this.hitT=0.18; this.state='hit'; this.stateT=0;
    if(this.health<=0){ this.alive=false; this.state='dead'; return true; }
    return false;
  }
  update(dt, game){
    this.stateT+=dt; if(this.hitT>0) this.hitT-=dt; if(this.cooldown>0) this.cooldown-=dt;
    // override in subclass
  }
}
class Gravebound extends Enemy{
  constructor(x,y,scale=1){
    super(x,y,'gravebound');
    const c=CONFIG.enemies.gravebound;
    this.health=c.health*scale; this.maxHealth=this.health; this.damage=c.damage*scale; this.speed=c.speed; this.bodyRadius=c.bodyRadius;
    this.windupDur=c.attackWindup; this.activeDur=c.attackActive;
  }
  update(dt, game){
    super.update(dt, game);
    const player=game.player;
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    if(this.state==='hit'){
      if(this.stateT>0.2){ this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0; }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='windup'){
      if(this.stateT>=this.windupDur){
        this.state='attack'; this.stateT=0;
        // attack hit check
        if(Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y)<52 && player.iFrameT<=0) {
          const res=player.takeDamage(this.damage, this);
          if(res==='hit'){ game.onPlayerHit(this.damage); }
          else if(res==='perfect'){ game.onPerfectDodge(); }
        }
        game.spawnHitEffect(this.x, this.y, false);
      }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='attack'){
      if(this.stateT>0.25){ this.state='cooldown'; this.stateT=0; this.cooldown=0.9+Math.random()*0.5; }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='cooldown'){
      if(this.cooldown<=0){ this.state='idle'; }
      return;
    }
    // idle/chase
    if(dist<480){
      this.intent = dist<70?1:0;
      if(dist<58){
        this.state='windup'; this.stateT=0;
        game.triggerTactical(0.42, this);
      }else{
        const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
        this.facing=ang;
        this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt;
        this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt;
      }
    }else{
      // wander tiny
      this.x+=Math.sin(this.stateT*0.7)*4*dt;
    }
  }
}
class CinderWisp extends Enemy{
  constructor(x,y,scale=1){
    super(x,y,'wisp');
    const c=CONFIG.enemies.wisp;
    this.health=c.health*scale; this.maxHealth=this.health; this.damage=c.damage*scale; this.speed=c.speed; this.bodyRadius=c.bodyRadius;
    this.windupDur=c.windup; this.projSpeed=c.projSpeed; this.range=c.range;
    this.floatOffset=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
  }
  update(dt, game){
    super.update(dt, game);
    const player=game.player;
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    this.floatOffset+=dt*2.1;
    this.y+=Math.sin(this.floatOffset)*10*dt;
    if(this.state==='hit'){ if(this.stateT>0.18){ this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0;} return; }
    if(this.state==='windup'){
      if(this.stateT>=this.windupDur){
        this.state='attack'; this.stateT=0;
        const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
        const vx=Math.cos(ang)*this.projSpeed, vy=Math.sin(ang)*this.projSpeed;
        game.spawnProjectile(this.x, this.y, vx, vy, this.damage, this);
        this.cooldown=1.4;
        this.state='cooldown';
      }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='cooldown'){ if(this.cooldown<=0) this.state='idle'; else this.cooldown-=dt; return; }
    // keep distance
    if(dist<this.range){
      this.intent=1;
      if(dist<180){
        // flee
        const ang=Math.atan2(this.y-player.y, this.x-player.x);
        this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt;
        this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt;
      }else if(dist>220){
        const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
        this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.7;
        this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.7;
      }
      if(this.cooldown<=0 && dist<260){
        this.state='windup'; this.stateT=0;
        game.triggerTactical(0.46, this);
      }
    }else{
      const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
      this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.5;
      this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.5;
    }
  }
}
class HookHound extends Enemy{
  constructor(x,y,scale=1){
    super(x,y,'hound');
    const c=CONFIG.enemies.hound;
    this.health=c.health*scale; this.maxHealth=this.health; this.damage=c.damage*scale;
    this.speed=c.speed; this.chargeSpeed=c.chargeSpeed; this.bodyRadius=c.bodyRadius;
    this.windupDur=c.windup; this.telegraphDur=c.chargeTelegraph;
    this.chargeDir={x:0,y:0};
  }
  update(dt, game){
    super.update(dt, game);
    const player=game.player;
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    if(this.state==='hit'){ if(this.stateT>0.18){ this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0;} return; }
    if(this.state==='telegraph'){
      if(this.stateT>=this.telegraphDur){
        this.state='charging'; this.stateT=0;
        const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
        this.chargeDir={x:Math.cos(ang), y:Math.sin(ang)};
      }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='charging'){
      this.x+=this.chargeDir.x*this.chargeSpeed*dt;
      this.y+=this.chargeDir.y*this.chargeSpeed*dt;
      // check hit player during charge
      if(Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y)<30 && player.iFrameT<=0){
        const res=player.takeDamage(this.damage, this);
        if(res==='hit') game.onPlayerHit(this.damage);
        else if(res==='perfect') game.onPerfectDodge();
        this.state='cooldown'; this.cooldown=1.0; this.stateT=0;
        return;
      }
      if(this.stateT>0.55){ this.state='cooldown'; this.cooldown=0.9; this.stateT=0; }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='cooldown'){ if(this.cooldown<=0) this.state='idle'; else this.cooldown-=dt; return; }
    // chase fast, occasional telegraph
    if(dist<520){
      this.intent=dist<220?1:0;
      const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
      this.facing=ang;
      this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt;
      this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt;
      if(dist<200 && this.cooldown<=0){
        this.state='telegraph'; this.stateT=0;
        this.telegraphLine={ x1:this.x, y1:this.y, x2:player.x, y2:player.y };
        game.triggerTactical(0.5, this);
      }
    }
  }
}
class MourningKnight extends Enemy{
  constructor(x,y,scale=1){
    super(x,y,'knight');
    const c=CONFIG.enemies.knight;
    this.health=c.health*scale; this.maxHealth=this.health; this.damage=c.damage*scale;
    this.speed=c.speed; this.bodyRadius=c.bodyRadius; this.isElite=true;
    this.windupDur=c.windup; this.blockArc=c.blockArc; this.vulnAfter=c.vulnerableAfter;
    this.blocking=true; this.vulnerableT=0;
  }
  takeDamage(d, fromPlayer, isCrit=false){
    if(this.blocking && this.vulnerableT<=0){
      const angToPlayer=Math.atan2(fromPlayer.y-this.y, fromPlayer.x-this.x);
      let diff=Math.abs(angToPlayer-this.facing);
      diff=((diff+Math.PI)%(Math.PI*2))-Math.PI; diff=Math.abs(diff);
      if(diff < this.blockArc/2){
        // blocked
        return false;
      }
    }
    return super.takeDamage(d, fromPlayer, isCrit);
  }
  update(dt, game){
    super.update(dt, game);
    const player=game.player;
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    if(this.vulnerableT>0) this.vulnerableT-=dt;
    this.blocking=this.vulnerableT<=0;
    if(this.state==='hit'){ if(this.stateT>0.22){ this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0;} return; }
    if(this.state==='windup'){
      if(this.stateT>=this.windupDur){
        this.state='slam'; this.stateT=0;
        // aoe slam
        if(dist<88 && player.iFrameT<=0){
          const res=player.takeDamage(this.damage, this);
          if(res==='hit') game.onPlayerHit(this.damage);
          else if(res==='perfect') game.onPerfectDodge();
        }
        game.spawnShockwave(this.x, this.y, 0, 96, this.damage*0.7);
        this.vulnerableT=this.vulnAfter;
        this.cooldown=1.6;
        this.state='cooldown';
      }
      return;
    }
    if(this.state==='cooldown'){ if(this.cooldown<=0) this.state='idle'; else this.cooldown-=dt; return; }
    if(dist<560){
      this.intent=1;
      const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
      this.facing=ang;
      if(dist>72){
        this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt;
        this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt;
      }else if(this.cooldown<=0){
        this.state='windup'; this.stateT=0;
        game.triggerTactical(0.6, this);
      }
    }
  }
}
class AshenLord extends Enemy{
  constructor(x,y,scale=1){
    super(x,y,'boss');
    const c=CONFIG.enemies.boss;
    this.health=c.health*scale; this.maxHealth=this.health; this.damage=c.damage*scale;
    this.speed=c.speed; this.bodyRadius=c.bodyRadius; this.isElite=true;
    this.phase=0; this.phaseT=0; this.patternCooldown=c.patternCooldown;
    this.patternT=0;
  }
  update(dt, game){
    super.update(dt, game);
    const player=game.player;
    this.phaseT+=dt; this.patternT+=dt;
    if(this.state==='hit'){ if(this.stateT>0.18){ this.state='idle'; this.stateT=0;} return; }
    if(this.phaseT>CONFIG.enemies.boss.phaseDur){
      this.phase=(this.phase+1)%3; this.phaseT=0;
      game.centerMsg(['⟡ Ember Unbound ⟡','⟡ Graviton Collapse ⟡','⟡ Vessel Call ⟡'][this.phase], 1.2);
    }
    const dist=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
    if(this.patternT>=this.patternCooldown){
      this.patternT=0;
      if(this.phase===0){ // ring
        game.triggerTactical(0.7, this);
        for(let i=0;i<CONFIG.enemies.boss.ringCount;i++){
          const ang=(Math.PI*2*i)/CONFIG.enemies.boss.ringCount + this.phaseT;
          game.spawnProjectile(this.x, this.y, Math.cos(ang)*155, Math.sin(ang)*155, this.damage*0.65, this);
        }
      }else if(this.phase===1){ // slam shockwave
        game.triggerTactical(0.7, this);
        this.state='windup'; this.stateT=0;
        setTimeout(()=>{
          if(!this.alive) return;
          game.spawnShockwave(this.x, this.y, 40, CONFIG.enemies.boss.shockRadius, this.damage);
          // check player
          const d=Math.hypot(player.x-this.x, player.y-this.y);
          if(d<CONFIG.enemies.boss.shockRadius+20 && player.iFrameT<=0){
            const res=player.takeDamage(this.damage, this);
            if(res==='hit') game.onPlayerHit(this.damage); else if(res==='perfect') game.onPerfectDodge();
          }
        }, 620);
      }else{
        // summon
        game.triggerTactical(0.65, this);
        const ang=Math.random()*Math.PI*2;
        for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
          const nx=this.x+Math.cos(ang + i*Math.PI)*80;
          const ny=this.y+Math.sin(ang + i*Math.PI)*80;
          const g=new Gravebound(nx,ny,0.85); g.health*=0.8; g.maxHealth=g.health;
          game.enemies.push(g);
        }
      }
    }
    // movement
    if(dist>90){
      const ang=Math.atan2(player.y-this.y, player.x-this.x);
      this.facing=ang;
      this.x+=Math.cos(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.6;
      this.y+=Math.sin(ang)*this.speed*dt*0.6;
    }
    this.intent=1;
  }
}
// ---------------- Dungeon Generation ----------------
const ROOM_TYPES=['combat','combat','combat','treasure','healing','event'];
class Room{
  constructor(id, type, seed, depth, rng){
    this.id=id; this.type=type; this.seed=seed; this.depth=depth; this.rng=rng;
    this.w= 860 + rng()*220;
    this.h= 520 + rng()*160;
    this.x=0; this.y=0; // world bounds? centered at 0? We'll use room local 0,0 with bounds -w/2..w/2
    this.enemies=[]; this.coins=[]; this.cleared=false; this.visited=false;
    this.exits=[]; // {dir, nextId}
    this.treasures=[];
    this.spawnDefs=[];
    this.generate();
  }
  generate(){
    const r=this.rng;
    if(this.type==='combat'){
      const count = 2 + Math.floor(r()*2) + Math.floor(this.depth*0.36);
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const pick = r();
        let kind='gravebound';
        if(pick<0.18) kind='hound';
        else if(pick<0.33) kind='wisp';
        else if(pick<0.47 && this.depth>2) kind='knight';
        else kind='gravebound';
        const an = r()*Math.PI*2; const rad= 70 + r()*180;
        this.spawnDefs.push({kind, x: Math.cos(an)*rad, y: Math.sin(an)*rad});
      }
    }else if(this.type==='elite'){
      this.spawnDefs.push({kind:'knight', x:0, y:-30});
      this.spawnDefs.push({kind:'gravebound', x:70, y:50});
    }else if(this.type==='boss'){
      this.spawnDefs.push({kind:'boss', x:0, y:0});
    }else if(this.type==='treasure'){
      this.spawnDefs.push({kind:'coins', count: 14+Math.floor(r()*12)});
    }else if(this.type==='healing'){
      this.spawnDefs.push({kind:'heal'});
    }
  }
  instantiateEnemies(gameScale){
    const out=[];
    for(const d of this.spawnDefs){
      if(d.kind==='gravebound') out.push(new Gravebound(d.x,d.y, gameScale));
      else if(d.kind==='wisp') out.push(new CinderWisp(d.x,d.y, gameScale));
      else if(d.kind==='hound') out.push(new HookHound(d.x,d.y, gameScale));
      else if(d.kind==='knight') out.push(new MourningKnight(d.x,d.y, gameScale));
      else if(d.kind==='boss') out.push(new AshenLord(d.x,d.y, gameScale));
    }
    return out;
  }
}
class Dungeon{
  constructor(seedStr, rng){
    this.seed=seedStr; this.rng=rng;
    this.rooms=[]; this.roomMap={};
    this.currentIdx=0;
    this.generate();
  }
  generate(){
    const r=this.rng;
    const total=CONFIG.dungeon.roomsPerRun;
    const types=[];
    types.push('start');
    for(let i=1;i<total-1;i++){
      if(CONFIG.dungeon.eliteFloors.includes(i)) types.push('elite');
      else if(i%CONFIG.dungeon.treasureEvery===0) types.push(r()<0.55?'treasure':'healing');
      else types.push( ROOM_TYPES[Math.floor(r()*ROOM_TYPES.length)] );
    }
    types.push('boss');
    for(let i=0;i<total;i++){
      const room=new Room(i, types[i], this.seed+'-'+i, i, r);
      this.rooms.push(room);
    }
    // link linearly + branching visuals (for simplicity, 1 exit forward, but we display choice of left/right that both go to same next but different reward)
    for(let i=0;i<total-1;i++){
      this.rooms[i].exits.push({ dir: (r()<0.5?'north':'east'), nextId:i+1, label: r()<0.5?'Ember Path':'Ruin Path' });
      if(i<total-2 && r()<0.42){
        this.rooms[i].exits.push({ dir:'west', nextId:i+1, label:'Veiled Path', altReward:true });
      }
    }
  }
  getCurrent(){ return this.rooms[this.currentIdx]; }
}
// ---------------- Game Main ----------------
const GameStates={ BOOT:'boot', START:'start', HOW:'how', HIGHSCORE:'hs', PLAY:'play', TACTICAL:'tactical', PAUSE:'pause', REWARD:'reward', TRANSITION:'transition', GAMEOVER:'gameover', VICTORY:'victory' };
class Game{
  constructor(canvas, input, audio, particlePool, floatingPool){
    this.canvas=canvas; this.ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
    this.input=input; this.audio=audio; this.particles=particlePool; this.floating=floatingPool;
    this.state=GameStates.BOOT;
    this.seedStr='ASH-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7).toUpperCase();
    this.rng=makeRNG(this.seedStr);
    this.dungeon=null;
    this.player=null;
    this.enemies=[];
    this.projectiles=[];
    this.coins=[];
    this.shockwaves=[];
    this.camera={ x:0, y:0, zoom:1, shake:0, shakeT:0 };
    this.time=0; this.roomTime=0; this.runStart=0;
    this.tacticalT=0; this.tacticalActive=false; this.timeScale=1;
    this.hitStop=0;
    this.score=0;
    this.floor=1;
    this.roomClearBonusGiven=false;
    this.noDamageRoom=true;
    this.enemiesKilledRun=0;
    this.coinsCollectedRun=0;
    this.performance={ fps:60, last:performance.now(), frames:0, fpsT:0 };
    this.reducedMotion=false;
    this.bgEmbers=[];
    this.spawnBgEmbers();
    this.roomBackgroundCache=null;
    this.chooseSeed('random');
    this.debug=false;
    this.roomTransitionT=0;
    this.pendingReward=false;
    this.rewardOptions=[];
    this.bossDefeated=false;
    this.styleRank='C';
  }
  spawnBgEmbers(){
    for(let i=0;i<60;i++) this.bgEmbers.push({ x:(Math.random()-0.5)*1400, y:(Math.random()-0.5)*800, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*18, vy:-8-Math.random()*22, size:0.8+Math.random()*2.2, alpha:0.3+Math.random()*0.5 });
  }
  chooseSeed(mode, custom){
    if(mode==='random'){
      this.seedStr='ASH-'+Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7).toUpperCase() + '-'+Math.floor(Math.random()*999);
    }else if(mode==='daily'){
      const d=new Date(); this.seedStr=`DAILY-${d.getFullYear()}${d.getMonth()+1}${d.getDate()}`;
    }else if(custom){ this.seedStr=custom; }
    this.rng=makeRNG(this.seedStr);
    document.getElementById('startSeedLabel').textContent=this.seedStr;
    document.getElementById('seedDisplay').textContent=this.seedStr;
    document.getElementById('goSeed').textContent=this.seedStr;
    // store
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ashen_seed', this.seedStr); }catch{}
  }
  startRun(){
    this.rng=makeRNG(this.seedStr);
    this.dungeon=new Dungeon(this.seedStr, this.rng);
    this.floor=1;
    this.score=0; this.time=0; this.runStart=performance.now();
    this.roomClearBonusGiven=false; this.noDamageRoom=true; this.enemiesKilledRun=0; this.coinsCollectedRun=0;
    this.player=new Player(0, 80);
    this.enterRoom(0);
    this.state=GameStates.PLAY;
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='flex';
    this.hideAllPanels();
    this.centerMsg('⟡ Enter the Ashen ⟡', 1.3);
    this.audio.play('roomClear');
  }
  enterRoom(idx){
    this.dungeon.currentIdx=idx;
    const room=this.dungeon.getCurrent();
    room.visited=true;
    this.enemies=room.instantiateEnemies(1 + idx*0.14);
    this.projectiles=[]; this.coins=[]; this.shockwaves=[];
    this.roomTime=0; this.roomClearBonusGiven=false; this.noDamageRoom=true;
    this.player.x=0; this.player.y=120;
    this.roomTransitionT=0.6;
    this.centerMsg(`▸ ${room.type.toUpperCase()} — Floor ${idx+1}`, 1.0);
    if(room.type==='boss'){
      this.audio.play('bossEnter');
      this.centerMsg('⟡ ASHEN LORD AWAKENS ⟡', 2.0);
    }
    // coin spawns for treasure
    if(room.type==='treasure'){
      const def=room.spawnDefs.find(d=>d.kind==='coins');
      const count=def?def.count:12;
      for(let i=0;i<count;i++){
        const a=Math.random()*Math.PI*2, rad=Math.random()*140;
        this.coins.push(new Coin(Math.cos(a)*rad, Math.sin(a)*rad, 1));
      }
    }
    document.getElementById('roomCount').textContent= (idx+1)+'/'+this.dungeon.rooms.length;
    document.getElementById('floorCount').textContent= (Math.floor(idx/3)+1)+'-'+((idx%3)+1);
  }
  performPlayerAttack(player){
    const facing=player.facing;
    const range=CONFIG.player.attackRange;
    const width=CONFIG.player.attackWidth;
    let hitSomething=false;
    let kills=0;
    for(const e of this.enemies){
      if(!e.alive) continue;
      const dx=e.x-player.x, dy=e.y-player.y;
      const dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
      const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
      let diff=Math.abs(ang-facing); diff=((diff+Math.PI)%(Math.PI*2))-Math.PI; diff=Math.abs(diff);
      const inArc = diff < (Math.PI*0.45);
      const inRange = dist < range;
      if(inArc && inRange){
        const isCrit = Math.random()< (player.hasRelic('desperate')?0.18:0.09) || diff<0.12;
        const dmg = CONFIG.player.attackDamage * (isCrit?1.85:1) * (player.hasRelic('ember_heart') && player.combo>2 ?1.35:1) * (player.hasRelic('desperate') && player.health<player.maxHealth*0.45?1.28:1);
        const dead=e.takeDamage(dmg, player, isCrit);
        hitSomething=true;
        this.particles.spawn({ type:'spark', x:e.x, y:e.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*120, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*120, life:0.28, alpha:1 });
        this.floating.spawn(e.x, e.y-18, isCrit?`CRIT ${dmg|0}`:`${dmg|0}`, isCrit?'#ffdf5a':'#fff', 0.7);
        this.camera.shake+= isCrit?CONFIG.juice.shakeHeavy:CONFIG.juice.shakeLight;
        this.hitStop = isCrit?CONFIG.juice.hitStopHeavy:CONFIG.juice.hitStopFrames;
        if(dead){
          this.onEnemyKilled(e);
          kills++;
        }
      }
    }
    // shard secondary
    if(player.hasShard){
      const vx=Math.cos(facing)*420, vy=Math.sin(facing)*420;
      this.spawnProjectile(player.x, player.y, vx, vy, CONFIG.player.attackDamage*0.6, player, 1.2, 7, true);
    }
    if(hitSomething){
      this.audio.play(kills?'hit':'attack');
      this.triggerTactical(0.32, null);
    }
  }
  performAbility(){
    const p=this.player;
    const facing=p.facing;
    const range=CONFIG.player.abilityRange;
    const arc=CONFIG.player.abilityArc;
    let hits=0;
    for(const e of this.enemies){
      if(!e.alive) continue;
      const dx=e.x-p.x, dy=e.y-p.y;
      const dist=Math.hypot(dx,dy);
      const ang=Math.atan2(dy,dx);
      let diff=Math.abs(ang-facing); diff=((diff+Math.PI)%(Math.PI*2))-Math.PI; diff=Math.abs(diff);
      if(dist<range && diff<arc/2){
        const dead=e.takeDamage(CONFIG.player.abilityDamage, p);
        this.particles.spawn({ type:'ember', x:e.x, y:e.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*80, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*80, life:0.6, size:3, alpha:0.9, color:'#ff9a42' });
        this.floating.spawn(e.x, e.y, `${CONFIG.player.abilityDamage|0}`, '#ff8a2e', 0.7);
        if(dead) this.onEnemyKilled(e);
        hits++;
      }
    }
    // visual shockwave
    this.shockwaves.push({ x:p.x, y:p.y, r:0, maxR:range, t:0, life:0.38, type:'ability' });
    this.camera.shake+= CONFIG.juice.shakeHeavy;
    this.hitStop= CONFIG.juice.hitStopHeavy;
    this.triggerTactical(0.68, null);
    this.audio.play('reward');
    if(hits>0) p.combo=Math.min(12, p.combo+1), p.comboT=4.0;
  }
  spawnProjectile(x,y,vx,vy,damage,owner,life=2.8,rad=6,isShard=false){
    if(this.projectiles.length>=CONFIG.perf.maxProjectiles) return;
    const p=new Projectile(x,y,vx,vy,damage,owner,life,rad);
    p.isShard=isShard;
    this.projectiles.push(p);
  }
  spawnShockwave(x,y,inner,maxR,damage){
    this.shockwaves.push({ x,y, inner, r:inner||0, maxR, t:0, life:0.45, damage, type:'enemy' });
    this.camera.shake+= CONFIG.juice.shakeLight;
  }
  spawnHitEffect(x,y,crit){
    for(let i=0;i<(crit?10:5);i++){
      this.particles.spawn({ type:'spark', x, y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*200, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*200, life:0.32+Math.random()*0.18, alpha:0.95 });
    }
  }
  triggerTactical(duration, enemy){
    if(this.tacticalActive) return;
    this.tacticalActive=true; this.tacticalT=duration;
    this.timeScale=0.18;
    document.getElementById('tacticalOverlay').classList.add('on');
    if(enemy) enemy.intent=2;
    setTimeout(()=>{
      // visuals will clear via update
    }, duration*1000);
  }
  onEnemyKilled(enemy){
    this.enemiesKilledRun++;
    this.score+= enemy.isElite?CONFIG.score.eliteKill:CONFIG.score.enemyKill;
    this.score+= Math.floor(this.player.combo*CONFIG.score.enemyKill*CONFIG.score.comboMult);
    this.player.combo=Math.min(18, this.player.combo+1);
    this.player.comboT= this.player.hasRelic('combo_sigil')?5.0:3.2;
    this.player.enemiesDefeated++;
    if(enemy.isElite && this.player.hasRelic('knight_oath')){
      this.player.health=Math.min(this.player.maxHealth, this.player.health+18);
      this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-30, '+18 HP', '#5aff8a', 0.9);
    }
    // drop coins
    const coinCount = enemy.isElite? (5+Math.floor(this.rng()*5)) : (2+Math.floor(this.rng()*3));
    for(let i=0;i<coinCount;i++){
      this.coins.push(new Coin(enemy.x + (Math.random()-0.5)*28, enemy.y+(Math.random()-0.5)*28, 1));
    }
    if(enemy.type==='boss'){
      this.bossDefeated=true;
      this.score+=CONFIG.score.bossKill;
      this.victory();
      return;
    }
    this.audio.play('hit');
    // room clear check
    const aliveEnemies=this.enemies.filter(e=>e.alive).length;
    if(aliveEnemies===0){
      this.onRoomClear();
    }
  }
  onRoomClear(){
    if(this.roomClearBonusGiven) return;
    this.roomClearBonusGiven=true;
    const timeBonus = this.roomTime<24? CONFIG.score.fastClearBonus : 0;
    const noDmg = this.noDamageRoom? CONFIG.score.noDamageRoom : 0;
    this.score+= timeBonus+noDmg;
    this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-50, `CLEAR +${timeBonus+noDmg}`, '#ffdf5a', 1.0);
    this.audio.play('roomClear');
    // if healing room, heal
    const room=this.dungeon.getCurrent();
    if(room.type==='healing'){
      this.player.health=Math.min(this.player.maxHealth, this.player.health+36);
      this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-30, '+36 HP', '#5aff8a', 0.9);
    }
    if(room.type==='treasure' || room.type==='event'){
      // extra coins already spawned
    }
    // decide next: if not last, show reward choice
    if(room.type!=='boss'){
      this.pendingReward=true;
      setTimeout(()=> this.showReward(), 600);
    }else{
      // boss -> victory handled elsewhere
    }
    // visual
    for(let i=0;i<22;i++) this.particles.spawn({ type:'ember', x:this.player.x, y:this.player.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*160, vy:(Math.random()-1)*180, life:0.9+Math.random()*0.6, size:2+Math.random()*2, alpha:0.9 });
  }
  showReward(){
    const options=[];
    const pool=[...CONFIG.relics];
    // shuffle via rng
    for(let i=pool.length-1;i>0;i--){ const j=Math.floor(this.rng()* (i+1)); [pool[i],pool[j]]=[pool[j],pool[i]]; }
    // avoid duplicates owned
    const filtered=pool.filter(r=>!this.player.hasRelic(r.id));
    const choices=filtered.slice(0,3);
    if(choices.length===0) { this.nextRoom(); return; }
    this.rewardOptions=choices;
    this.state=GameStates.REWARD;
    const grid=document.getElementById('rewardGrid');
    grid.innerHTML='';
    choices.forEach((rel,i)=>{
      const div=document.createElement('div');
      div.className='reward-card';
      div.innerHTML=`<div class="rarity ${rel.rarity}">${rel.rarity} • ${i+1}</div><h4>${rel.icon} ${rel.name}</h4><p>${rel.desc}</p>`;
      div.onclick=()=> this.chooseReward(i);
      grid.appendChild(div);
    });
    document.getElementById('rewardPanel').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='none';
  }
  chooseReward(idx){
    const rel=this.rewardOptions[idx];
    if(!rel) return;
    this.player.addRelic(rel);
    this.score+= 120;
    this.audio.play('reward');
    this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-40, rel.name, '#ffab5c', 1.0);
    document.getElementById('rewardPanel').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='flex';
    this.state=GameStates.PLAY;
    this.nextRoomPrompt();
  }
  nextRoomPrompt(){
    // show door choices if any
    const room=this.dungeon.getCurrent();
    if(room.exits.length>1){
      // simple choice: first exit
      this.centerMsg(`Paths: ${room.exits.map(e=>e.label).join(' • ')} — Press E to continue`, 2.2);
    }else{
      this.centerMsg('Press E for next chamber', 1.6);
    }
    this.pendingNext=true;
  }
  nextRoom(){
    const curIdx=this.dungeon.currentIdx;
    if(curIdx+1>=this.dungeon.rooms.length){
      this.victory(); return;
    }
    this.enterRoom(curIdx+1);
    this.pendingNext=false;
    this.pendingReward=false;
  }
  onPlayerHit(damage){
    this.noDamageRoom=false;
    this.player.combo=Math.max(0, this.player.combo-2);
    this.camera.shake+= CONFIG.juice.shakeLight;
    this.hitStop= CONFIG.juice.hitStopFrames;
  }
  onPerfectDodge(){
    this.score+=CONFIG.score.perfectDodge;
    this.player.combo=Math.min(18, this.player.combo+2);
    this.player.comboT=4.5;
    // perfect gives brief invuln extension and ember
    this.player.iFrameT=Math.max(this.player.iFrameT, 0.18);
    this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-36, 'PERFECT!', '#5affff', 0.9);
    this.audio.play('dodge');
    for(let i=0;i<12;i++) this.particles.spawn({ type:'ember', x:this.player.x, y:this.player.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*140, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*140, life:0.5, size:2.2, alpha:0.85 });
  }
  centerMsg(text, duration=1.5){
    const el=document.getElementById('centerText');
    el.textContent=text;
    el.style.opacity='1';
    el.style.transform='translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1.06)';
    setTimeout(()=>{ el.style.opacity='0'; el.style.transform='translate(-50%,-50%) scale(1)'; }, duration*1000);
  }
  // ----- persistence -----
  loadHighScores(){
    try{
      const raw=localStorage.getItem('ashen_descent_scores');
      if(!raw) return [];
      const arr=JSON.parse(raw);
      if(!Array.isArray(arr)) return [];
      return arr;
    }catch(e){ return []; }
  }
  saveHighScore(entry){
    const scores=this.loadHighScores();
    scores.push(entry);
    scores.sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score);
    const top=scores.slice(0,10);
    try{ localStorage.setItem('ashen_descent_scores', JSON.stringify(top)); }catch{}
    return top;
  }
  computeStyleRank(){
    const s=this.score; const t=this.time;
    let rank='C';
    if(s>1200) rank='B';
    if(s>2800) rank='A';
    if(s>5200) rank='S';
    if(s>8200 && this.enemiesKilledRun>22) rank='SS';
    if(t<200 && s>3800) rank+='+Swift';
    return rank;
  }
  gameOver(cause='Fallen in ash'){
    this.state=GameStates.GAMEOVER;
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='none';
    const goPanel=document.getElementById('gameOverPanel');
    goPanel.style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('goTitle').textContent= cause.includes('Victory')?'Lost?':'Fallen';
    document.getElementById('goCause').textContent=cause;
    document.getElementById('goScore').textContent=this.score|0;
    document.getElementById('goFloor').textContent=(this.dungeon?this.dungeon.currentIdx+1:1);
    document.getElementById('goEnemies').textContent=this.enemiesKilledRun;
    document.getElementById('goTime').textContent=this.formatTime(this.time);
    document.getElementById('goCoins').textContent=this.coinsCollectedRun;
    document.getElementById('goSeed').textContent=this.seedStr;
    const style=this.computeStyleRank();
    document.getElementById('goStyle').textContent=style;
    const best=this.loadHighScores()[0]?.score||0;
    document.getElementById('goBest').textContent=best;
    // save
    this.saveHighScore({ score:this.score|0, floor:this.dungeon?this.dungeon.currentIdx+1:1, enemies:this.enemiesKilledRun, time:this.time|0, date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10), seed:this.seedStr, style:style });
  }
  victory(){
    this.state=GameStates.VICTORY;
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='none';
    document.getElementById('victoryPanel').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('vicScore').textContent=this.score|0;
    document.getElementById('vicTime').textContent=this.formatTime(this.time);
    document.getElementById('vicFloor').textContent=(this.dungeon?this.dungeon.rooms.length:9)+' cleared';
    document.getElementById('vicStyle').textContent=this.computeStyleRank();
    this.saveHighScore({ score:this.score|0, floor:this.dungeon.rooms.length, enemies:this.enemiesKilledRun, time:this.time|0, date:new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10), seed:this.seedStr, style:this.computeStyleRank()+'-VICTORY', victory:true });
    this.audio.play('roomClear');
    for(let i=0;i<80;i++) this.particles.spawn({ type:'ember', x:(Math.random()-0.5)*400, y:(Math.random()-0.5)*260, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*120, vy:-20-Math.random()*120, life:1.2+Math.random()*1.2, size:1.8+Math.random()*3, alpha:0.9 });
  }
  formatTime(sec){ const m=Math.floor(sec/60), s=Math.floor(sec%60); return `${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(s).padStart(2,'0')}`; }
  // ----- main update -----
  update(dt){
    // perf
    this.performance.frames++; this.performance.fpsT+=dt;
    if(this.performance.fpsT>0.4){
      this.performance.fps=Math.round(this.performance.frames/this.performance.fpsT);
      this.performance.frames=0; this.performance.fpsT=0;
    }
    if(this.hitStop>0){ this.hitStop--; return; }
    const scaledDt=dt*this.timeScale;
    // tactical decay
    if(this.tacticalActive){
      this.tacticalT-=dt;
      if(this.tacticalT<=0){
        this.tacticalActive=false; this.timeScale=1;
        document.getElementById('tacticalOverlay').classList.remove('on');
      }
    }
    if(this.state===GameStates.PLAY){
      this.time+=scaledDt;
      this.roomTime+=scaledDt;
      // input update handled outside
      if(this.player) this.player.update(scaledDt, this.input, this);
      // clamp player to room bounds
      const room=this.dungeon?.getCurrent();
      if(room && this.player){
        const halfW=room.w/2 - 28, halfH=room.h/2 - 28;
        this.player.x=Math.max(-halfW, Math.min(halfW, this.player.x));
        this.player.y=Math.max(-halfH, Math.min(halfH, this.player.y));
      }
      // enemies
      for(const e of this.enemies){ if(e.alive) e.update(scaledDt, this); }
      this.enemies=this.enemies.filter(e=>e.alive || e.state!=='dead' || e.type==='boss'); // keep boss briefly? simplify remove dead eventually
      // but actually purge dead after short time -> handle in loop
      this.enemies=this.enemies.filter(e=> !(e.state==='dead' && e.stateT>0.6) || e.type==='boss'); // boss stays for check
      this.enemies=this.enemies.filter(e=> e.alive || e.health>0); // final purge
      // Wait: need proper dead purge: if alive false remove, except we already killed have handled kill
      this.enemies=this.enemies.filter(e=> e.alive);
      // projectiles
      for(const pr of this.projectiles){
        pr.update(scaledDt);
        // collide with player
        if(pr.owner!==this.player){
          if(Math.hypot(pr.x-this.player.x, pr.y-this.player.y)<this.player.radius+pr.radius){
            if(this.player.iFrameT<=0){
              const res=this.player.takeDamage(pr.damage, pr.owner);
              if(res==='hit') this.onPlayerHit(pr.damage);
              else if(res==='perfect') this.onPerfectDodge();
            }
            pr.alive=false;
            this.spawnHitEffect(pr.x, pr.y, false);
          }
        }else{
          // player's shard vs enemies
          for(const e of this.enemies){
            if(!e.alive) continue;
            if(Math.hypot(pr.x-e.x, pr.y-e.y)<e.bodyRadius+pr.radius){
              const dead=e.takeDamage(pr.damage, this.player);
              if(dead) this.onEnemyKilled(e);
              pr.alive=false;
              this.particles.spawn({ type:'ember', x:e.x, y:e.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*60, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*60, life:0.4, size:2 });
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        // wall bounds kill
        if(room){
          const hw=room.w/2, hh=room.h/2;
          if(Math.abs(pr.x)>hw+40 || Math.abs(pr.y)>hh+40) pr.alive=false;
        }
      }
      this.projectiles=this.projectiles.filter(p=>p.alive);
      // coins
      for(const c of this.coins){
        if(c.update(scaledDt, this.player)){
          this.coinsCollectedRun++; this.player.coins++; this.score+=CONFIG.score.coin;
          this.audio.play('coin');
          this.floating.spawn(c.x, c.y, `+${CONFIG.score.coin}`, '#ffdf5a', 0.6);
          if(this.player.hasRelic('coin_plate') && this.player.coins%22===0){
            this.player.armor+=8;
            this.floating.spawn(this.player.x, this.player.y-20, '+8 Armor', '#5aff8a', 0.8);
          }
          // ember trail
          for(let i=0;i<3;i++) this.particles.spawn({ type:'coinSpark', x:c.x, y:c.y, vx:(Math.random()-0.5)*90, vy:(Math.random()-0.5)*90, life:0.38 });
        }
      }
      this.coins=this.coins.filter(c=>!c.collected);
      // shockwaves
      for(const sh of this.shockwaves){
        sh.t+=scaledDt; sh.r+= (sh.maxR - (sh.inner||0)) * (scaledDt / sh.life);
        if(sh.t>=sh.life) sh.alive=false;
      }
      this.shockwaves=this.shockwaves.filter(s=>s.t<s.life);
      // particles
      this.particles.update(scaledDt);
      this.floating.update(scaledDt);
      // camera follow
      if(this.player){
        const targetX=this.player.x + this.input.moveVector().x*88;
        const targetY=this.player.y + this.input.moveVector().y*56;
        this.camera.x += (targetX - this.camera.x)* (1 - Math.pow(0.02, scaledDt*60));
        this.camera.y += (targetY - this.camera.y)* (1 - Math.pow(0.02, scaledDt*60));
      }
      // camera shake decay
      if(this.camera.shake>0){
        this.camera.shakeT+=scaledDt*30;
        this.camera.shake*=Math.pow(0.001, scaledDt); // fast decay
        if(this.camera.shake<0.1) this.camera.shake=0;
      }
      // handle pending next room via E
      if(this.pendingNext && (this.input.keys['e'] || this.input.keyDown['e'])){ this.nextRoom(); }
      // check player death
      if(this.player && !this.player.alive){
        this.gameOver('Crushed beneath ash & vow');
      }
    }
    // bg embers always
    for(const em of this.bgEmbers){
      em.x+=em.vx*dt; em.y+=em.vy*dt;
      if(em.y<-500){ em.y=500; em.x=(Math.random()-0.5)*1400; }
      if(em.x<-800) em.x=800; if(em.x>800) em.x=-800;
    }
    this.updateHUD();
  }
  updateHUD(){
    if(!this.player || this.state!==GameStates.PLAY) return;
    const hpPct=Math.max(0, this.player.health/this.player.maxHealth);
    document.getElementById('hpFill').style.width=(hpPct*100)+'%';
    document.getElementById('coinCount').textContent=this.player.coins;
    document.getElementById('scoreCount').textContent=this.score|0;
    document.getElementById('bestScore').textContent=this.loadHighScores()[0]?.score||0;
    document.getElementById('timeCount').textContent=this.formatTime(this.time);
    const comboFill=document.getElementById('comboFill');
    comboFill.style.width=(this.player.combo>0? Math.min(100, (this.player.comboT/ (this.player.hasRelic('combo_sigil')?5.0:3.2))*100) : 0)+'%';
    document.getElementById('comboText').textContent=this.player.combo>1?`COMBO x${this.player.combo} ${this.player.hasRelic('combo_sigil')?'⚡':''}`:'';
    const abil=document.getElementById('abilityStatus');
    if(this.player.abilityCooldown>0) abil.textContent=`ABILITY ${this.player.abilityCooldown.toFixed(1)}s [K]`, abil.style.color='#8a839a';
    else abil.textContent=`ABILITY READY [K]`, abil.style.color='var(--ember3)';
    // relics
    const row=document.getElementById('relicRow');
    row.innerHTML='';
    this.player.relics.forEach(r=>{
      const d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='relic-badge'; d.textContent=r.icon; d.title=r.name+': '+r.desc;
      row.appendChild(d);
    });
  }
  hideAllPanels(){
    ['startPanel','howPanel','scorePanel','pausePanel','rewardPanel','gameOverPanel','victoryPanel'].forEach(id=>{
      document.getElementById(id).style.display='none';
    });
  }
  showStart(){
    this.state=GameStates.START;
    this.hideAllPanels();
    document.getElementById('startPanel').style.display='block';
    document.getElementById('hud').style.display='none';
    // render bg without player
  }
  togglePause(){
    if(this.state===GameStates.PLAY){
      this.state=GameStates.PAUSE;
      document.getElementById('pausePanel').style.display='block';
      this.audio.play('pause');
    }else if(this.state===GameStates.PAUSE){
      this.state=GameStates.PLAY;
      document.getElementById('pausePanel').style.display='none';
    }
  }
  // ----- Rendering -----
  draw(){
    const ctx=this.ctx;
    const W=this.canvas.width, H=this.canvas.height;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);
    // background gothic painterly base
    // dark gradient already via css but draw over for richness
    const grad=ctx.createRadialGradient(W*0.5,H*0.35,W*0.1, W*0.5,H*0.35, W*1.2);
    grad.addColorStop(0,'#1f1c2a'); grad.addColorStop(0.25,'#16131e'); grad.addColorStop(0.6,'#0f0d15'); grad.addColorStop(1,'#08060d');
    ctx.fillStyle=grad; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    // vignette
    const vig=ctx.createRadialGradient(W*0.5,H*0.5, W*0.3, W*0.5,H*0.5, W*0.9);
    vig.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)'); vig.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0.72)');
    ctx.fillStyle=vig; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    // fog layers drift
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.06;
    for(let i=0;i<2;i++){
      const off=(this.time*6+i*200)%W;
      const g=ctx.createLinearGradient(off,0,off+400,0);
      g.addColorStop(0,'rgba(107,91,138,0)'); g.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(107,91,138,0.6)'); g.addColorStop(1,'rgba(107,91,138,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=g;
      ctx.fillRect(0, H*0.15+i*120, W, 90);
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    // if no dungeon yet, draw title atmospheric
    if(!this.dungeon || !this.player){
      this.drawTitleScene(ctx);
      ctx.restore();
      return;
    }
    // camera transform
    ctx.translate(W/2, H/2);
    const shakeX=(this.reducedMotion?0:(Math.sin(this.camera.shakeT*1.7)*this.camera.shake));
    const shakeY=(this.reducedMotion?0:(Math.cos(this.camera.shakeT*2.3)*this.camera.shake*0.6));
    ctx.translate(shakeX, shakeY);
    ctx.scale(this.camera.zoom, this.camera.zoom);
    ctx.translate(-this.camera.x, -this.camera.y);
    // draw room
    this.drawRoom(ctx, this.dungeon.getCurrent());
    // shockwaves
    for(const sh of this.shockwaves){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha= (1 - sh.t/sh.life) * (this.reducedMotion?0.35:0.7);
      ctx.strokeStyle= sh.type==='ability'? '#ff7a2e' : '#ff3b3b';
      ctx.lineWidth= sh.type==='ability'? 3 : 4;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sh.x, sh.y, sh.r, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
      if(!this.reducedMotion){
        ctx.globalAlpha*=0.22;
        ctx.fillStyle=sh.type==='ability'?'rgba(255,122,46,0.25)':'rgba(255,59,59,0.18)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sh.x, sh.y, sh.r, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // coins
    for(const c of this.coins){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(c.x, c.y);
      ctx.rotate(this.time*2 + c.x*0.01);
      // coin visual: layered gothic coin with ember core
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffdf5a';
      ctx.shadowColor='#ff7a2e'; ctx.shadowBlur=6;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0,6,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      ctx.fillStyle='#d8a42a';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0,3.5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // afterimages for player dodge
    if(!this.reducedMotion){
      for(const tr of this.player.trail){
        const a=tr.t/0.22;
        ctx.globalAlpha=a*0.28;
        ctx.fillStyle='#9a8aff';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(tr.x, tr.y, 14, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    }
    // enemies
    for(const e of this.enemies) this.drawEnemy(ctx, e);
    // projectiles
    for(const pr of this.projectiles){
      ctx.save();
      if(pr.isShard){
        ctx.fillStyle='#ffab5c'; ctx.shadowColor='#ff7a2e'; ctx.shadowBlur=8;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pr.x,pr.y, pr.radius,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }else if(pr.owner && pr.owner.type==='wisp'){
        ctx.fillStyle='#ff6a3a'; ctx.shadowColor='#ff3b3b'; ctx.shadowBlur=10;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pr.x,pr.y,6,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
        // telegraph trail
        ctx.globalAlpha=0.18; ctx.fillStyle='#ff7a2e';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pr.x-pr.vx*0.05, pr.y-pr.vy*0.05, 3,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }else{
        ctx.fillStyle='#c07aff'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(pr.x,pr.y,5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // player
    if(this.player) this.drawPlayer(ctx, this.player);
    // particles (world space)
    this.particles.draw(ctx);
    // attack windup indicator
    if(this.player && this.player.attackPhase==='windup'){
      const prog=this.player.attackT/CONFIG.player.attackWindup;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.26*prog;
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffdf5a';
      ctx.beginPath();
      const ang=this.player.facing;
      ctx.moveTo(this.player.x, this.player.y);
      ctx.arc(this.player.x, this.player.y, CONFIG.player.attackRange, ang-Math.PI*0.32, ang+Math.PI*0.32);
      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // tactical intent icons + projected zones
    if(this.tacticalActive){
      for(const e of this.enemies){
        if(e.intent){
          // intent icon above
          ctx.save();
          ctx.fillStyle= e.intent===2?'#ff3b3b':'#ffb84a';
          ctx.font='bold 22px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
          ctx.fillText(e.intent===2?'⚠︎':'◍', e.x, e.y - e.bodyRadius - 22);
          // projected zone
          ctx.globalAlpha=0.16;
          if(e.type==='gravebound'){
            ctx.fillStyle='#ff3b3b';
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(e.x, e.y, 52, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
          }else if(e.type==='hound' && e.telegraphLine){
            ctx.strokeStyle='#ff3b3b'; ctx.lineWidth=3; ctx.setLineDash([8,6]);
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(e.telegraphLine.x1, e.telegraphLine.y1); ctx.lineTo(e.telegraphLine.x2, e.telegraphLine.y2); ctx.stroke();
            ctx.setLineDash([]);
          }else if(e.type==='wisp'){
            ctx.strokeStyle='#ff7a2e'; ctx.lineWidth=2;
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(e.x, e.y, 18, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
          }else if(e.type==='knight'){
            ctx.fillStyle='#ff3b3b';
            ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(e.x, e.y, 88, -0.6, 0.6); ctx.lineTo(e.x, e.y); ctx.fill();
          }
          ctx.restore();
        }
      }
    }
    ctx.restore(); // camera
    // floating texts in screen space? Keep world but after camera restore, need transform again? We'll draw in world already via pool that uses world coords but we need second transform? Simpler draw floating after camera with manual conversion
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(W/2, H/2);
    ctx.translate(shakeX, shakeY);
    ctx.scale(this.camera.zoom, this.camera.zoom);
    ctx.translate(-this.camera.x, -this.camera.y);
    this.floating.draw(ctx);
    ctx.restore();
    // foreground embers screen-space
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.72;
    for(const em of this.bgEmbers){
      const sx= W*0.5 + em.x*0.28 + Math.sin(this.time*0.3+em.x)*10;
      const sy= H*0.5 + em.y*0.28;
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,${120+Math.floor(em.alpha*80)},${20+Math.floor(em.alpha*20)},${em.alpha*0.6})`;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx,sy, em.size,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,122,46,${em.alpha*0.09})`;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(sx,sy, em.size*3,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // vignette final
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.14)';
    ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    const finalVig=ctx.createRadialGradient(W*0.5,H*0.5,0,W*0.5,H*0.5,W*0.95);
    finalVig.addColorStop(0,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    finalVig.addColorStop(0.68,'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    finalVig.addColorStop(1,'rgba(0,0,0,0.58)');
    ctx.fillStyle=finalVig; ctx.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
    // debug
    if(this.debug){
      document.getElementById('debugOverlay').style.display='block';
      document.getElementById('debugOverlay').textContent=`FPS ${this.performance.fps} | Enemies ${this.enemies.length} | Particles ${this.particles.active.length} | Seed ${this.seedStr} | Pos ${this.player?this.player.x.toFixed(0)+','+this.player.y.toFixed(0):'-'} | State ${this.state}`;
    }
  }
  drawRoom(ctx, room){
    const W=room.w, H=room.h;
    // stone floor base with procedural mottled
    ctx.save();
    // base
    ctx.fillStyle='#15131c';
    ctx.fillRect(-W/2-20, -H/2-20, W+40, H+40);
    // floor tiles procedural
    const tile=48;
    for(let x=-W/2; x<W/2; x+=tile){
      for(let y=-H/2; y<H/2; y+=tile){
        const n= (Math.sin(x*0.02+this.seedStr.charCodeAt(0))*0.5+0.5)*0.4 + (Math.cos(y*0.03)*0.3+0.3);
        const shade= 0.86 + n*0.28;
        const cVal= Math.floor(26*shade);
        ctx.fillStyle=`rgb(${cVal+8},${cVal},${cVal+12})`;
        // stone brick with crack variation
        ctx.fillRect(x+1, y+1, tile-2, tile-2);
        // mortar speckle
        if((x+y)%(tile*2)===0){
          ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.14)';
          ctx.fillRect(x, y, tile, 2);
          ctx.fillRect(x, y, 2, tile);
        }
        // ember dust
        if((x*7+y*13)%79===0){
          ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,122,46,0.06)';
          ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(x+12, y+22, 2,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
        }
      }
    }
    // walls: thick stone border with layered depth
    const wallThick=32;
    // top wall
    const wallGradTop=ctx.createLinearGradient(0,-H/2-wallThick,0,-H/2);
    wallGradTop.addColorStop(0,'#2a2738'); wallGradTop.addColorStop(1,'#1e1c28');
    ctx.fillStyle=wallGradTop;
    ctx.fillRect(-W/2-wallThick, -H/2-wallThick, W+wallThick*2, wallThick);
    // bottom
    ctx.fillRect(-W/2-wallThick, H/2, W+wallThick*2, wallThick);
    // left
    ctx.fillRect(-W/2-wallThick, -H/2-wallThick, wallThick, H+wallThick*2);
    // right
    ctx.fillRect(W/2, -H/2-wallThick, wallThick, H+wallThick*2);
    // torch sconces
    const torchPositions=[{x:-W/2+18, y:-H/2+44},{x:W/2-18, y:-H/2+44},{x:-W/2+18, y:H/2-44},{x:W/2-18, y:H/2-44}];
    for(const tp of torchPositions){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(tp.x, tp.y);
      // sconce shadow
      ctx.fillStyle='#0c0a12'; ctx.fillRect(-4,-10,8,18);
      // flame flicker
      const flick= 0.85+Math.sin(this.time*7 + tp.x)*0.15;
      ctx.shadowColor='#ff7a2e'; ctx.shadowBlur=14*flick;
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,${120+flick*60|0},${20}|0}`;
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.9;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,-8); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(6*flick, -2, 0, 8); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-6*flick, -2, 0, -8); ctx.fill();
      ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      // light pool on floor
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.12*flick;
      const lightGrad=ctx.createRadialGradient(0,20,0,0,20,88);
      lightGrad.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,122,46,0.55)'); lightGrad.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,122,46,0)');
      ctx.fillStyle=lightGrad;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,20,88,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // room type sigil in center faint
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.06;
    ctx.fillStyle='#6b5b8a';
    ctx.font='900 86px ui-serif, serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
    const sigil={combat:'⚔', treasure:'◈', healing:'✦', event:'❖', elite:'♔', boss:'☠', start:'⟡'}[room.type]||'·';
    ctx.fillText(sigil, 0, 18);
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    // exits glowing doors
    for(const ex of room.exits){
      ctx.save();
      let dx=0, dy=0;
      if(ex.dir==='north'){ dx=0; dy=-H/2; }
      else if(ex.dir==='east'){ dx=W/2; dy=0; }
      else if(ex.dir==='west'){ dx=-W/2; dy=0; }
      ctx.translate(dx,dy);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,122,46,0.22)'; ctx.shadowColor='#ff7a2e'; ctx.shadowBlur=16;
      ctx.fillRect(-28,-6,56,12);
      if(ex.dir==='north') ctx.fillRect(-28,-22,56,18);
      if(ex.dir==='east') ctx.fillRect(16,-28,18,56);
      if(ex.dir==='west') ctx.fillRect(-34,-28,18,56);
      ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffab5c'; ctx.font='12px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText(ex.label||'', 0, ex.dir==='north'? -28 : 36);
      ctx.restore();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }
  drawPlayer(ctx, p){
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);
    ctx.rotate(p.facing);
    // shadow
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.38)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(0, 12, 18, 8, 0, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    // cloak gothic with ember trim
    const isHit= p.hitT>0 && Math.floor(p.hitT*18)%2===0;
    const baseCol= isHit? '#ff9a9a' : (p.iFrameT>0? '#b0aaff' : '#e8dcc8');
    // body silhouette
    ctx.fillStyle=baseCol;
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(16,0); // front
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(8, -14, -10, -12);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-14,0,-10,12);
    ctx.quadraticCurveTo(8,14,16,0);
    ctx.fill();
    // ember trim edge
    ctx.strokeStyle= p.hasRelic('desperate')&&p.health<p.maxHealth*0.45? '#ff3b3b' : '#ff7a2e';
    ctx.lineWidth= (p.hasRelic('desperate')&&p.health<p.maxHealth*0.45?2.4:1.2);
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.86;
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    // facing indicator arrow (subtle)
    ctx.fillStyle='#ffab5c';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(18,0); ctx.lineTo(12,-5); ctx.lineTo(12,5); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
    // weapon during attack
    if(p.attackPhase!=='none'){
      const prog= p.attackPhase==='windup'? p.attackT/CONFIG.player.attackWindup : (p.attackPhase==='active'?1: 1-p.attackT/CONFIG.player.attackRecovery);
      ctx.rotate(prog*0.9 -0.45);
      ctx.strokeStyle='#ffdf9a'; ctx.lineWidth=3;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.lineTo(CONFIG.player.attackRange*prog, 0); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,223,154,0.3)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, CONFIG.player.attackRange*prog, -0.5, 0.5); ctx.fill();
    }
    // ability visual ring
    if(p.state==='ability'){
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.55*(1-p.stateT/0.32);
      ctx.strokeStyle='#ff7a2e'; ctx.lineWidth=3;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, 26 + p.stateT*260, -CONFIG.player.abilityArc/2, CONFIG.player.abilityArc/2); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.18*(1-p.stateT/0.32);
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff7a2e'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.arc(0,0, CONFIG.player.abilityRange, -CONFIG.player.abilityArc/2, CONFIG.player.abilityArc/2); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha=1;
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // i-frame halo
    if(p.iFrameT>0 && !this.reducedMotion){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha=0.28* (p.iFrameT/CONFIG.player.iFrameDur);
      ctx.strokeStyle='#9a8aff'; ctx.lineWidth=2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(p.x, p.y, 22, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.restore();
    }
    // health mini above
    if(p.health<p.maxHealth){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.55)'; ctx.fillRect(p.x-18, p.y-30, 36, 4);
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff3b3b'; ctx.fillRect(p.x-18, p.y-30, 36*(p.health/p.maxHealth), 4);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  drawEnemy(ctx, e){
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(e.x, e.y);
    ctx.rotate(e.facing||0);
    let body='#3a384a';
    if(e.type==='gravebound') body='#4a445a';
    if(e.type==='wisp') body='#6a5a3a';
    if(e.type==='hound') body='#5a3a2a';
    if(e.type==='knight') body='#2a2a4a';
    if(e.type==='boss') body='#1a1228';
    const hitFlash= e.hitT>0 && Math.floor(e.hitT*20)%2===0;
    if(hitFlash) body='#ffcccc';
    // shadow
    ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.36)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(2, 10, e.bodyRadius*0.9, e.bodyRadius*0.45, 0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
    // body stylized not rectangle
    if(e.type==='gravebound'){
      // hunched figure with gothic cloak
      ctx.fillStyle=body;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, e.bodyRadius, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#1a1820'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-4,-2, e.bodyRadius*0.6, 0, Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); // hood shadow
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff7a2e'; ctx.globalAlpha=0.8;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(6,-4, 2.5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(6,2,2.5,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); // eyes ember
      ctx.globalAlpha=1;
      // arm swing indicator if windup
      if(e.state==='windup'){
        const prog=e.stateT/e.windupDur;
        ctx.strokeStyle='#ff3b3b'; ctx.lineWidth=2+prog*2;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.lineTo( (e.bodyRadius+12)*prog, -2); ctx.stroke();
      }
    }else if(e.type==='wisp'){
      // floating wisp with ember core and trailing parchment
      const bob=Math.sin(e.floatOffset)*4;
      ctx.translate(0,bob);
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(255,122,46,0.18)'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, e.bodyRadius*1.9,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffae5a'; ctx.shadowColor='#ff7a2e'; ctx.shadowBlur=12;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, e.bodyRadius*0.7,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.shadowBlur=0;
      // rag tatters
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(201,185,154,0.6)'; ctx.lineWidth=1;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-6,6); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-2,14,-8,20); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(6,6); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(2,14,8,20); ctx.stroke();
      if(e.state==='windup'){
        ctx.strokeStyle='#ff7a2e'; ctx.lineWidth=1.5; ctx.setLineDash([4,4]);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, 12+e.stateT*30,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.setLineDash([]);
      }
    }else if(e.type==='hound'){
      // sleek hound silhouette
      ctx.fillStyle=body;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(4,0, e.bodyRadius+4, e.bodyRadius*0.7,0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#2a2018'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.ellipse(-6,0, e.bodyRadius*0.8, e.bodyRadius*0.6,0,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ff5a2a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(12, -2, 2,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      if(e.state==='telegraph'){
        ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,59,59,0.55)'; ctx.lineWidth=2; ctx.setLineDash([6,5]);
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,0); ctx.lineTo(80,0); ctx.stroke(); ctx.setLineDash([]);
      }
    }else if(e.type==='knight'){
      ctx.fillStyle= e.blocking? '#3a3a5a' : '#2a2a3a';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.roundRect(-e.bodyRadius, -e.bodyRadius, e.bodyRadius*2, e.bodyRadius*2, 6); ctx.fill();
      // shield frontal if blocking
      if(e.blocking){
        ctx.fillStyle='#5a5a7a'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc( e.bodyRadius*0.6,0, 18, -0.9, 0.9); ctx.fill();
        ctx.strokeStyle='#8a8ab0'; ctx.lineWidth=1.5; ctx.stroke();
      }else{
        // vulnerable cracked
        ctx.strokeStyle='#ff3b3b'; ctx.lineWidth=1.2;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-8,-8); ctx.lineTo(8,8); ctx.moveTo(8,-8); ctx.lineTo(-8,8); ctx.stroke();
      }
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffab5c'; ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,-4, 3,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill(); // visor ember
    }else if(e.type==='boss'){
      // Ashen Lord massive silhouette with multiple layers, cloak, crown, ember veins
      ctx.fillStyle='#1a1222';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, e.bodyRadius,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#2a1a3a';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,-6, e.bodyRadius*0.82,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.fill();
      // crown horns
      ctx.fillStyle='#3a2a1a';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(-12,-e.bodyRadius); ctx.lineTo(-16,-e.bodyRadius-14); ctx.lineTo(-8,-e.bodyRadius-6); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(12,-e.bodyRadius); ctx.lineTo(16,-e.bodyRadius-14); ctx.lineTo(8,-e.bodyRadius-6); ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      // ember veins pulse
      const pulse=0.6+Math.sin(this.time*3+ e.id)*0.4;
      ctx.fillStyle=`rgba(255,122,46,${0.6*pulse})`;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0,-10); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(8,0,0,16); ctx.quadraticCurveTo(-8,0,0,-10); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle='#ffdf9a'; ctx.font='bold 9px sans-serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
      ctx.fillText('BOSS', 0, -e.bodyRadius-18);
      // phase indicator ring
      ctx.strokeStyle='rgba(255,122,46,0.32)'; ctx.lineWidth=2;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0,0, e.bodyRadius+12+Math.sin(this.time*2)*2,0,Math.PI*2); ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // health bar for elites/boss
    if(e.isElite || e.type==='boss'){
      ctx.save();
      ctx.fillStyle='rgba(0,0,0,0.5)'; ctx.fillRect(e.x-26, e.y- e.bodyRadius-16, 52, 5);
      ctx.fillStyle= e.type==='boss'? '#ff7a2e' : '#ff3b3b';
      ctx.fillRect(e.x-26, e.y- e.bodyRadius-16, 52* Math.max(0, e.health/e.maxHealth), 5);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }
  drawTitleScene(ctx){
    // atmospheric pillars, ember rain, title hint
    const W=this.canvas.width, H=this.canvas.height;
    // pillars silhouette
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.12;
    ctx.fillStyle='#2a2738';
    for(let i=0;i<4;i++){
      const x= W*0.15 + i*W*0.22 + Math.sin(this.time*0.2+i)*10;
      ctx.fillRect(x, H*0.2, 34, H*0.6);
      // capital
      ctx.fillRect(x-8, H*0.2-12, 50, 14);
    }
    ctx.restore();
    // floating glyphs
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha=0.06;
    ctx.fillStyle='#ff7a2e';
    ctx.font='64px serif'; ctx.textAlign='center';
    ctx.fillText('⟡', W*0.5 + Math.sin(this.time*0.4)*12, H*0.36);
    ctx.restore();
  }
}

const canvas=document.getElementById('gameCanvas');
const input=new InputManager(canvas);
